@7 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [he put forth]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 1"} [a parable]{strong="G3850" x-morph="robinson: N-ASF 6"} [to]{strong="G4314" x-morph="robinson: PREP 3"} [those which]{strong="G3588" x-morph="robinson: T-APM 4"} [were bidden]{strong="G2564" x-morph="robinson: V-RPP-APM 5"}, [when he marked]{strong="G1907" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NSM 7"} [how]{strong="G4459" x-morph="robinson: ADV 8"} [they chose out]{strong="G1586" x-morph="robinson: V-IMI-3P 11"} [the chief rooms]{strong="G3588 G4411" x-morph="robinson: T-APF robinson: N-APF 9 10"}; [saying]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NSM 12"} [unto]{strong="G4314" x-morph="robinson: PREP 13"} [them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-APM 14"}, 
@8 [When thou art bidden of any man to a wedding, sit not down in the highest room; lest a more honourable man than thou be bidden of him;]{.wj} 
@9 [And he that bade thee and him come and say to thee, Give this man place; and thou begin with shame to take the lowest room.]{.wj} 
@10 [But when thou art bidden, go and sit down in the lowest room; that when he that bade thee cometh, he may say unto thee, Friend, go up higher: then shalt thou have worship in the presence of them that sit at meat with thee.]{.wj} 
@11 [For whosoever exalteth himself shall be abased; and he that humbleth himself shall be exalted.]{.wj} 

@12 [Then]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [said he]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 1"} [also]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 3"} [to him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 6"} [that]{strong="G3588" x-morph="robinson: T-DSM 4"} [bade him]{strong="G2564" x-morph="robinson: V-RAP-DSM 5"}, [When thou makest a dinner or a supper, call not thy friends, nor thy brethren, neither thy kinsmen, nor thy rich neighbours; lest they also bid thee again, and a recompence be made thee.]{.wj} 
@13 [But when thou makest a feast, call the poor, the maimed, the lame, the blind:]{.wj} 
@14 [And thou shalt be blessed; for they cannot recompense thee: for thou shalt be recompensed at the resurrection of the just.]{.wj} 

@15 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [when one]{strong="G5100" x-morph="robinson: X-NSM 3"} [of them that]{strong="G3588" x-morph="robinson: T-GPM 4"} [sat at meat with him]{strong="G4873" x-morph="robinson: V-PNP-GPM 5"} [heard]{strong="G191" x-morph="robinson: V-AAP-NSM 1"} [these things]{strong="G5023" x-morph="robinson: D-APN 6"}, [he said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 7"} [unto him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSM 8"}, [Blessed]{strong="G3107" x-morph="robinson: A-NSM 9"} is [he that]{strong="G3739" x-morph="robinson: R-NSM 10"} [shall eat]{strong="G5315" x-morph="robinson: V-FDI-3S 11"} [bread]{strong="G740" x-morph="robinson: N-ASM 12"} [in]{strong="G1722" x-morph="robinson: PREP 13"} [the kingdom]{strong="G3588 G932" x-morph="robinson: T-DSF robinson: N-DSF 14 15"} [of God]{strong="G3588 G2316" x-morph="robinson: T-GSM robinson: N-GSM 16 17"}. 
@16 [Then]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [said he]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 3"} [unto him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSM 4"}, [A certain man made a great supper, and bade many:]{.wj} 
@17 [And sent his servant at supper time to say to them that were bidden, Come; for all things are now ready.]{.wj} 
@18 [And they all with one consent began to make excuse. The first said unto him, I have bought a piece of ground, and I must needs go and see it: I pray thee have me excused.]{.wj} 
@19 [And another said, I have bought five yoke of oxen, and I go to prove them: I pray thee have me excused.]{.wj} 
@20 [And another said, I have married a wife, and therefore I cannot come.]{.wj} 
@21 [So that servant came, and shewed his lord these things. Then the master of the house being angry said to his servant, Go out quickly into the streets and lanes of the city, and bring in hither the poor, and the maimed, and the halt, and the blind.]{.wj} 
@22 [And the servant said, Lord, it is done as thou hast commanded, and yet there is room.]{.wj} 
@23 [And the lord said unto the servant, Go out into the highways and hedges, and compel them to come in, that my house may be filled.]{.wj} 
@24 [For I say unto you, That none of those men which were bidden shall taste of my supper.]{.wj} 

@25 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [there went]{strong="G4848" x-morph="robinson: V-INI-3P 1"} [great]{strong="G4183" x-morph="robinson: A-NPM 5"} [multitudes]{strong="G3793" x-morph="robinson: N-NPM 4"} [with]{strong="G4848" x-morph="robinson: V-INI-3P 1"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSM 3"}: [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 6"} [he turned]{strong="G4762" x-morph="robinson: V-2APP-NSM 7"}, [and said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 8"} [unto]{strong="G4314" x-morph="robinson: PREP 9"} [them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-APM 10"}, 
@26 [If any man come to me, and hate not his father, and mother, and wife, and children, and brethren, and sisters, yea, and his own life also, he cannot be my disciple.]{.wj} 
@27 [And whosoever doth not bear his cross, and come after me, cannot be my disciple.]{.wj} 
@28 [For which of you, intending to build a tower, sitteth not down first, and counteth the cost, whether he have sufficient to finish it?]{.wj} 
@29 [Lest haply, after he hath laid the foundation, and is not able to finish it, all that behold it begin to mock him,]{.wj} 
@30 [Saying, This man began to build, and was not able to finish.]{.wj} 
@31 [Or what king, going to make war against another king, sitteth not down first, and consulteth whether he be able with ten thousand to meet him that cometh against him with twenty thousand?]{.wj} 
@32 [Or else, while the other is yet a great way off, he sendeth an ambassage, and desireth conditions of peace.]{.wj} 
@33 [So likewise, whosoever he be of you that forsaketh not all that he hath, he cannot be my disciple.]{.wj} 

@34 [Salt is good: but if the salt have lost his savour, wherewith shall it be seasoned?]{.wj} 
@35 [It is neither fit for the land, nor yet for the dunghill; but men cast it out. He that hath ears to hear, let him hear.]{.wj} 

# 15 
@1 [Then]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [drew near]{strong="G1448" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NPM 3"} [unto him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSM 4"} [all]{strong="G3956" x-morph="robinson: A-NPM 5"} [the publicans]{strong="G3588 G5057" x-morph="robinson: T-NPM robinson: N-NPM 6 7"} [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 8"} [sinners]{strong="G268" x-morph="robinson: A-NPM 10"} [for to hear]{strong="G191" x-morph="robinson: V-PAN 11"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GSM 12"}. 
@2 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [the Pharisees]{strong="G3588 G5330" x-morph="robinson: T-NPM robinson: N-NPM 3 4"} [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 5"} [scribes]{strong="G3588 G1122" x-morph="robinson: T-NPM robinson: N-NPM 6 7"} [murmured]{strong="G1234" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3P 2"}, [saying]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NPM 8"}, [This man]{strong="G3778" x-morph="robinson: D-NSM 10"} [receiveth]{strong="G4327" x-morph="robinson: V-PNI-3S 12"} [sinners]{strong="G268" x-morph="robinson: A-APM 11"}, [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 13"} [eateth]{strong="G4906" x-morph="robinson: V-PAI-3S 14"} [with them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 15"}. 

@3 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [he spake]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 1"} [this]{strong="G3778" x-morph="robinson: D-ASF 7"} [parable]{strong="G3588 G3850" x-morph="robinson: T-ASF robinson: N-ASF 5 6"} [unto]{strong="G4314" x-morph="robinson: PREP 3"} [them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-APM 4"}, [saying]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NSM 8"}, 
@4 [What man of you, having an hundred sheep, if he lose one of them, doth not leave the ninety and nine in the wilderness, and go after that which is lost, until he find it?]{.wj} 
@5 [And when he hath found it, he layeth it on his shoulders, rejoicing.]{.wj} 
@6 [And when he cometh home, he calleth together his friends and neighbours, saying unto them, Rejoice with me; for I have found my sheep which was lost.]{.wj} 
@7 [I say unto you, that likewise joy shall be in heaven over one sinner that repenteth, more than over ninety and nine just persons, which need no repentance.]{.wj} 

@8 [Either what woman having ten pieces of silver, if she lose one piece, doth not light a candle, and sweep the house, and seek diligently till she find it?]{.wj} 
@9 [And when she hath found it, she calleth her friends and her neighbours together, saying, Rejoice with me; for I have found the piece which I had lost.]{.wj} 
@10 [Likewise, I say unto you, there is joy in the presence of the angels of God over one sinner that repenteth.]{.wj} 

@11 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [he said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 1"}, [A certain man had two sons:]{.wj} 
@12 [And the younger of them said to his father, Father, give me the portion of goods that falleth to me. And he divided unto them his living.]{.wj} 
@13 [And not many days after the younger son gathered all together, and took his journey into a far country, and there wasted his substance with riotous living.]{.wj} 
@14 [And when he had spent all, there arose a mighty famine in that land; and he began to be in want.]{.wj} 
@15 [And he went and joined himself to a citizen of that country; and he sent him into his fields to feed swine.]{.wj} 
@16 [And he would fain have filled his belly with the husks that the swine did eat: and no man gave unto him.]{.wj} 
@17 [And when he came to himself, he said, How many hired servants of my father’s have bread enough and to spare, and I perish with hunger!]{.wj} 
@18 [I will arise and go to my father, and will say unto him, Father, I have sinned against heaven, and before thee,]{.wj} 
@19 [And am no more worthy to be called thy son: make me as one of thy hired servants.]{.wj} 
@20 [And he arose, and came to his father. But when he was yet a great way off, his father saw him, and had compassion, and ran, and fell on his neck, and kissed him.]{.wj} 
@21 [And the son said unto him, Father, I have sinned against heaven, and in thy sight, and am no more worthy to be called thy son.]{.wj} 
@22 [But the father said to his servants, Bring forth the best robe, and put it on him; and put a ring on his hand, and shoes on his feet:]{.wj} 
@23 [And bring hither the fatted calf, and kill it; and let us eat, and be merry:]{.wj} 
@24 [For this my son was dead, and is alive again; he was lost, and is found. And they began to be merry.]{.wj} 
@25 [Now his elder son was in the field: and as he came and drew nigh to the house, he heard musick and dancing.]{.wj} 
@26 [And he called one of the servants, and asked what these things meant.]{.wj} 
@27 [And he said unto him, Thy brother is come; and thy father hath killed the fatted calf, because he hath received him safe and sound.]{.wj} 
@28 [And he was angry, and would not go in: therefore came his father out, and intreated him.]{.wj} 
@29 [And he answering said to his father, Lo, these many years do I serve thee, neither transgressed I at any time thy commandment: and yet thou never gavest me a kid, that I might make merry with my friends:]{.wj} 
@30 [But as soon as this thy son was come, which hath devoured thy living with harlots, thou hast killed for him the fatted calf.]{.wj} 
@31 [And he said unto him, Son, thou art ever with me, and all that I have is thine.]{.wj} 
@32 [It was meet that we should make merry, and be glad: for this thy brother was dead, and is alive again; and was lost, and is found.]{.wj} 

# 16 
@1 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [he said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 1"} [also]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 3"} [unto]{strong="G4314" x-morph="robinson: PREP 4"} [his]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GSM 7"} [disciples]{strong="G3588 G3101" x-morph="robinson: T-APM robinson: N-APM 5 6"}, [There was a certain rich man, which had a steward; and the same was accused unto him that he had wasted his goods.]{.wj} 
@2 [And he called him, and said unto him, How is it that I hear this of thee? give an account of thy stewardship; for thou mayest be no longer steward.]{.wj} 
@3 [Then the steward said within himself, What shall I do? for my lord taketh away from me the stewardship: I cannot dig; to beg I am ashamed.]{.wj} 
@4 [I am resolved what to do, that, when I am put out of the stewardship, they may receive me into their houses.]{.wj} 
@5 [So he called every one of his lord’s debtors unto him, and said unto the first, How much owest thou unto my lord?]{.wj} 
@6 [And he said, An hundred measures of oil. And he said unto him, Take thy bill, and sit down quickly, and write fifty.]{.wj} 
@7 [Then said he to another, And how much owest thou? And he said, An hundred measures of wheat. And he said unto him, Take thy bill, and write fourscore.]{.wj} 
@8 [And the lord commended the unjust steward, because he had done wisely: for the children of this world are in their generation wiser than the children of light.]{.wj} 
@9 [And I say unto you, Make to yourselves friends of the mammon of unrighteousness; that, when ye fail, they may receive you into everlasting habitations.]{.wj} 
@10 [He that is faithful in that which is least is faithful also in much: and he that is unjust in the least is unjust also in much.]{.wj} 
@11 [If therefore ye have not been faithful in the unrighteous mammon, who will commit to your trust the true riches?]{.wj} 
@12 [And if ye have not been faithful in that which is another man’s, who shall give you that which is your own?]{.wj} 

@13 [No servant can serve two masters: for either he will hate the one, and love the other; or else he will hold to the one, and despise the other. Ye cannot serve God and mammon.]{.wj} 
@14 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [the Pharisees]{strong="G3588 G5330" x-morph="robinson: T-NPM robinson: N-NPM 6 7"} [also]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 5"}, [who were]{strong="G5225" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NPM 9"} [covetous]{strong="G5366" x-morph="robinson: A-NPM 8"}, [heard]{strong="G191" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3P 1"} [all]{strong="G3956" x-morph="robinson: A-APN 4"} [these things]{strong="G5023" x-morph="robinson: D-APN 3"}: [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 10"} [they derided]{strong="G1592" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3P 11"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 12"}. 
@15 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 2"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 3"}, [Ye are they which justify yourselves before men; but God knoweth your hearts: for that which is highly esteemed among men is abomination in the sight of God.]{.wj} 
@16 [The law and the prophets were until John: since that time the kingdom of God is preached, and every man presseth into it.]{.wj} 
@17 [And it is easier for heaven and earth to pass, than one tittle of the law to fail.]{.wj} 
@18 [Whosoever putteth away his wife, and marrieth another, committeth adultery: and whosoever marrieth her that is put away from her husband committeth adultery.]{.wj} 

@19 [There was a certain rich man, which was clothed in purple and fine linen, and fared sumptuously every day:]{.wj} 
@20 [And there was a certain beggar named Lazarus, which was laid at his gate, full of sores,]{.wj} 
@21 [And desiring to be fed with the crumbs which fell from the rich man’s table: moreover the dogs came and licked his sores.]{.wj} 
@22 [And it came to pass, that the beggar died, and was carried by the angels into Abraham’s bosom: the rich man also died, and was buried;]{.wj} 
@23 [And in hell he lift up his eyes, being in torments, and seeth Abraham afar off, and Lazarus in his bosom.]{.wj} 
@24 [And he cried and said, Father Abraham, have mercy on me, and send Lazarus, that he may dip the tip of his finger in water, and cool my tongue; for I am tormented in this flame.]{.wj} 
@25 [But Abraham said, Son, remember that thou in thy lifetime receivedst thy good things, and likewise Lazarus evil things: but now he is comforted, and thou art tormented.]{.wj} 
@26 [And beside all this, between us and you there is a great gulf fixed: so that they which would pass from hence to you cannot; neither can they pass to us, that would come from thence.]{.wj} 
@27 [Then he said, I pray thee therefore, father, that thou wouldest send him to my father’s house:]{.wj} 
@28 [For I have five brethren; that he may testify unto them, lest they also come into this place of torment.]{.wj} 
@29 [Abraham saith unto him, They have Moses and the prophets; let them hear them.]{.wj} 
@30 [And he said, Nay, father Abraham: but if one went unto them from the dead, they will repent.]{.wj} 
@31 [And he said unto him, If they hear not Moses and the prophets, neither will they be persuaded, though one rose from the dead.]{.wj} 

# 17 
@1 [Then]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [said he]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 1"} [unto]{strong="G4314" x-morph="robinson: PREP 3"} [the disciples]{strong="G3588 G3101" x-morph="robinson: T-APM robinson: N-APM 4 5"}, [It is impossible but that offences will come: but woe unto him, through whom they come!]{.wj} 
@2 [It were better for him that a millstone were hanged about his neck, and he cast into the sea, than that he should offend one of these little ones.]{.wj} 

@3 [Take heed to yourselves: If thy brother trespass against thee, rebuke him; and if he repent, forgive him.]{.wj} 
@4 [And if he trespass against thee seven times in a day, and seven times in a day turn again to thee, saying, I repent; thou shalt forgive him.]{.wj} 
@5 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [the apostles]{strong="G3588 G652" x-morph="robinson: T-NPM robinson: N-NPM 3 4"} [said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3P 2"} [unto the Lord]{strong="G3588 G2962" x-morph="robinson: T-DSM robinson: N-DSM 5 6"}, [Increase]{strong="G4369" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAM-2S 7"} [our]{strong="G2254" x-morph="robinson: P-1DP 8"} [faith]{strong="G4102" x-morph="robinson: N-ASF 9"}. 
@6 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [the Lord]{strong="G3588 G2962" x-morph="robinson: T-NSM robinson: N-NSM 3 4"} [said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 1"}, [If ye had faith as a grain of mustard seed, ye might say unto this sycamine tree, Be thou plucked up by the root, and be thou planted in the sea; and it should obey you.]{.wj} 
@7 [But which of you, having a servant plowing or feeding cattle, will say unto him by and by, when he is come from the field, Go and sit down to meat?]{.wj} 
@8 [And will not rather say unto him, Make ready wherewith I may sup, and gird thyself, and serve me, till I have eaten and drunken; and afterward thou shalt eat and drink?]{.wj} 
@9 [Doth he thank that servant because he did the things that were commanded him? I trow not.]{.wj} 
@10 [So likewise ye, when ye shall have done all those things which are commanded you, say, We are unprofitable servants: we have done that which was our duty to do.]{.wj} 

@11 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [it came to pass]{strong="G1096" x-morph="robinson: V-2ADI-3S 2"}, [as]{strong="G1722" x-morph="robinson: PREP 3"} [he]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-ASM 6"} [went]{strong="G4198" x-morph="robinson: V-PNN 5"} [to]{strong="G1519" x-morph="robinson: PREP 7"} [Jerusalem]{strong="G2419" x-morph="robinson: N-PRI 8"}, [that]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 9"} [he]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-NSM 10"} [passed]{strong="G1330" x-morph="robinson: V-INI-3S 11"} [through]{strong="G1223" x-morph="robinson: PREP 12"} [the midst]{strong="G3319" x-morph="robinson: A-GSN 13"} [of Samaria]{strong="G4540" x-morph="robinson: N-GSF 14"} [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 15"} [Galilee]{strong="G1056" x-morph="robinson: N-GSF 16"}. 
@12 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [as he]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GSM 3"} [entered]{strong="G1525" x-morph="robinson: V-PNP-GSM 2"} [into]{strong="G1519" x-morph="robinson: PREP 4"} [a certain]{strong="G5100" x-morph="robinson: X-ASF 5"} [village]{strong="G2968" x-morph="robinson: N-ASF 6"}, [there met]{strong="G528" x-morph="robinson: V-AAI-3P 7"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSM 8"} [ten]{strong="G1176" x-morph="robinson: A-NUI 9"} [men]{strong="G435" x-morph="robinson: N-NPM 11"} [that were lepers]{strong="G3015" x-morph="robinson: A-NPM 10"}, [which]{strong="G3739" x-morph="robinson: R-NPM 12"} [stood]{strong="G2476" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3P 13"} [afar off]{strong="G4207" x-morph="robinson: ADV 14"}: 
@13 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [they]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-NPM 2"} [lifted up]{strong="G142" x-morph="robinson: V-AAI-3P 3"} their [voices]{strong="G5456" x-morph="robinson: N-ASF 4"}, [and said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NPM 5"}, [Jesus]{strong="G2424" x-morph="robinson: N-VSM 6"}, [Master]{strong="G1988" x-morph="robinson: N-VSM 7"}, [have mercy]{strong="G1653" x-morph="robinson: V-AAM-2S 8"} [on us]{strong="G2248" x-morph="robinson: P-1AP 9"}. 
@14 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [when he saw]{strong="G1492" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAP-NSM 2"} them, [he said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 3"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 4"}, [Go shew yourselves unto the priests.]{.wj} [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 10"} [it came to pass, that]{strong="G1096" x-morph="robinson: V-2ADI-3S 11"}, [as]{strong="G1722" x-morph="robinson: PREP 12"} [they went]{strong="G5217" x-morph="robinson: V-PAN 14"}, [they]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-APM 15"} [were cleansed]{strong="G2511" x-morph="robinson: V-API-3P 16"}. 
@15 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [one]{strong="G1520" x-morph="robinson: A-NSM 1"} [of]{strong="G1537" x-morph="robinson: PREP 3"} [them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GPM 4"}, [when he saw]{strong="G1492" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAP-NSM 5"} [that]{strong="G3754" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 6"} [he was healed]{strong="G2390" x-morph="robinson: V-API-3S 7"}, [turned back]{strong="G5290" x-morph="robinson: V-AAI-3S 8"}, [and with]{strong="G3326" x-morph="robinson: PREP 9"} [a loud]{strong="G3173" x-morph="robinson: A-GSF 11"} [voice]{strong="G5456" x-morph="robinson: N-GSF 10"} [glorified]{strong="G1392" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NSM 12"} [God]{strong="G3588 G2316" x-morph="robinson: T-ASM robinson: N-ASM 13 14"}, 
@16 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [fell down]{strong="G4098" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 2"} [on]{strong="G1909" x-morph="robinson: PREP 3"} his [face]{strong="G4383" x-morph="robinson: N-ASN 4"} [at]{strong="G3844" x-morph="robinson: PREP 5"} [his]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-GSM 8"} [feet]{strong="G3588 G4228" x-morph="robinson: T-APM robinson: N-APM 6 7"}, [giving]{strong="G2168" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NSM 9"} [him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSM 10"} [thanks]{strong="G2168" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NSM 9"}: [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 11"} [he]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-NSM 12"} [was]{strong="G2258" x-morph="robinson: V-IXI-3S 13"} [a Samaritan]{strong="G4541" x-morph="robinson: N-NSM 14"}. 
@17 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [Jesus]{strong="G3588 G2424" x-morph="robinson: T-NSM robinson: N-NSM 3 4"} [answering]{strong="G611" x-morph="robinson: V-AOP-NSM 1"} [said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 5"}, [Were there not ten cleansed? but where are the nine?]{.wj} 
@18 [There are not found that returned to give glory to God, save this stranger.]{.wj} 
@19 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 2"} [unto him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSM 3"}, [Arise, go thy way: thy faith hath made thee whole.]{.wj} 

@20 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [when he was demanded]{strong="G1905" x-morph="robinson: V-APP-NSM 1"} [of]{strong="G5259" x-morph="robinson: PREP 3"} [the Pharisees]{strong="G3588 G5330" x-morph="robinson: T-GPM robinson: N-GPM 4 5"}, [when]{strong="G4219" x-morph="robinson: PRT-I 6"} [the kingdom]{strong="G3588 G932" x-morph="robinson: T-NSF robinson: N-NSF 8 9"} [of God]{strong="G3588 G2316" x-morph="robinson: T-GSM robinson: N-GSM 10 11"} [should come]{strong="G2064" x-morph="robinson: V-PNI-3S 7"}, [he answered]{strong="G611" x-morph="robinson: V-ADI-3S 12"} [them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 13"} [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 14"} [said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 15"}, [The kingdom of God cometh not with observation:]{.wj} 
@21 [Neither shall they say, Lo here! or, lo there! for, behold, the kingdom of God is within you.]{.wj} 
@22 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [he said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 1"} [unto]{strong="G4314" x-morph="robinson: PREP 3"} [the disciples]{strong="G3588 G3101" x-morph="robinson: T-APM robinson: N-APM 4 5"}, [The days will come, when ye shall desire to see one of the days of the Son of man, and ye shall not see it.]{.wj} 
@23 [And they shall say to you, See here; or, see there: go not after them, nor follow them.]{.wj} 
@24 [For as the lightning, that lighteneth out of the one part under heaven, shineth unto the other part under heaven; so shall also the Son of man be in his day.]{.wj} 
@25 [But first must he suffer many things, and be rejected of this generation.]{.wj} 
@26 [And as it was in the days of Noe, so shall it be also in the days of the Son of man.]{.wj} 
@27 [They did eat, they drank, they married wives, they were given in marriage, until the day that Noe entered into the ark, and the flood came, and destroyed them all.]{.wj} 
@28 [Likewise also as it was in the days of Lot; they did eat, they drank, they bought, they sold, they planted, they builded;]{.wj} 
@29 [But the same day that Lot went out of Sodom it rained fire and brimstone from heaven, and destroyed them all.]{.wj} 
@30 [Even thus shall it be in the day when the Son of man is revealed.]{.wj} 
@31 [In that day, he which shall be upon the housetop, and his stuff in the house, let him not come down to take it away: and he that is in the field, let him likewise not return back.]{.wj} 
@32 [Remember Lot’s wife.]{.wj} 
@33 [Whosoever shall seek to save his life shall lose it; and whosoever shall lose his life shall preserve it.]{.wj} 
@34 [I tell you, in that night there shall be two men in one bed; the one shall be taken, and the other shall be left.]{.wj} 
@35 [Two women shall be grinding together; the one shall be taken, and the other left.]{.wj} 
@36 [Two men shall be in the field; the one shall be taken, and the other left.]{.wj} 
@37 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [they answered]{strong="G611" x-morph="robinson: V-AOP-NPM 2"} [and said]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAI-3P 3"} [unto him]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DSM 4"}, [Where]{strong="G4226" x-morph="robinson: PRT-I 5"}, [Lord]{strong="G2962" x-morph="robinson: N-VSM 6"}? [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 8"} [he said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 9"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 10"}, [Wheresoever the body is, thither will the eagles be gathered together.]{.wj} 

# 18 
@1 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [he spake]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 1"} [a parable]{strong="G3850" x-morph="robinson: N-ASF 4"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 5"} to this end, [that men ought]{strong="G1163" x-morph="robinson: V-PQN 8"} [always]{strong="G3842" x-morph="robinson: ADV 9"} [to pray]{strong="G4336" x-morph="robinson: V-PNN 10"}, [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 11"} [not]{strong="G3361" x-morph="robinson: PRT-N 12"} [to faint]{strong="G1573" x-morph="robinson: V-PAN 13"}; 
@2 [Saying]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-NSM 1"}, [There was in a city a judge, which feared not God, neither regarded man:]{.wj} 
@3 [And there was a widow in that city; and she came unto him, saying, Avenge me of mine adversary.]{.wj} 
@4 [And he would not for a while: but afterward he said within himself, Though I fear not God, nor regard man;]{.wj} 
@5 [Yet because this widow troubleth me, I will avenge her, lest by her continual coming she weary me.]{.wj} 
@6 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [the Lord]{strong="G3588 G2962" x-morph="robinson: T-NSM robinson: N-NSM 3 4"} [said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 1"}, [Hear what the unjust judge saith.]{.wj} 
@7 [And shall not God avenge his own elect, which cry day and night unto him, though he bear long with them?]{.wj} 
@8 [I tell you that he will avenge them speedily. Nevertheless when the Son of man cometh, shall he find faith on the earth?]{.wj} 
@9 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [he spake]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 1"} [this]{strong="G3778" x-morph="robinson: D-ASF 19"} [parable]{strong="G3588 G3850" x-morph="robinson: T-ASF robinson: N-ASF 17 18"} [unto]{strong="G4314" x-morph="robinson: PREP 4"} [certain]{strong="G5100" x-morph="robinson: X-APM 5"} [which]{strong="G3588" x-morph="robinson: T-APM 6"} [trusted]{strong="G3982" x-morph="robinson: V-2RAP-APM 7"} [in]{strong="G1909" x-morph="robinson: PREP 8"} [themselves]{strong="G1438" x-morph="robinson: F-3DPM 9"} [that]{strong="G3754" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 10"} [they were]{strong="G1526" x-morph="robinson: V-PXI-3P 11"} [righteous]{strong="G1342" x-morph="robinson: A-NPM 12"}, [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 13"} [despised]{strong="G1848" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-APM 14"} [others]{strong="G3062" x-morph="robinson: A-APM 16"}: 
@10 [Two men went up into the temple to pray; the one a Pharisee, and the other a publican.]{.wj} 
@11 [The Pharisee stood and prayed thus with himself, God, I thank thee, that I am not as other men are, extortioners, unjust, adulterers, or even as this publican.]{.wj} 
@12 [I fast twice in the week, I give tithes of all that I possess.]{.wj} 
@13 [And the publican, standing afar off, would not lift up so much as his eyes unto heaven, but smote upon his breast, saying, God be merciful to me a sinner.]{.wj} 
@14 [I tell you, this man went down to his house justified rather than the other: for every one that exalteth himself shall be abased; and he that humbleth himself shall be exalted.]{.wj}

@12 [He said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 1"} [therefore]{strong="G3767" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"}, [A certain nobleman went into a far country to receive for himself a kingdom, and to return.]{.wj} 
@13 [And he called his ten servants, and delivered them ten pounds, and said unto them, Occupy till I come.]{.wj} 
@14 [But his citizens hated him, and sent a message after him, saying, We will not have this man to reign over us.]{.wj} 
@15 [And it came to pass, that when he was returned, having received the kingdom, then he commanded these servants to be called unto him, to whom he had given the money, that he might know how much every man had gained by trading.]{.wj} 
@16 [Then came the first, saying, Lord, thy pound hath gained ten pounds.]{.wj} 
@17 [And he said unto him, Well, thou good servant: because thou hast been faithful in a very little, have thou authority over ten cities.]{.wj} 
@18 [And the second came, saying, Lord, thy pound hath gained five pounds.]{.wj} 
@19 [And he said likewise to him, Be thou also over five cities.]{.wj} 
@20 [And another came, saying, Lord, behold, here is thy pound, which I have kept laid up in a napkin:]{.wj} 
@21 [For I feared thee, because thou art an austere man: thou takest up that thou layedst not down, and reapest that thou didst not sow.]{.wj} 
@22 [And he saith unto him, Out of thine own mouth will I judge thee, thou wicked servant. Thou knewest that I was an austere man, taking up that I laid not down, and reaping that I did not sow:]{.wj} 
@23 [Wherefore then gavest not thou my money into the bank, that at my coming I might have required mine own with usury?]{.wj} 
@24 [And he said unto them that stood by, Take from him the pound, and give it to him that hath ten pounds.]{.wj} 
@25 [(And they said unto him, Lord, he hath ten pounds.)]{.wj} 
@26 [For I say unto you, That unto every one which hath shall be given; and from him that hath not, even that he hath shall be taken away from him.]{.wj} 
@27 [But those mine enemies, which would not that I should reign over them, bring hither, and slay them before me.]{.wj}

@9 [Then]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [began he]{strong="G756" x-morph="robinson: V-ADI-3S 1"} [to speak]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAN 6"} [to]{strong="G4314" x-morph="robinson: PREP 3"} [the people]{strong="G3588 G2992" x-morph="robinson: T-ASM robinson: N-ASM 4 5"} [this]{strong="G3778" x-morph="robinson: D-ASF 9"} [parable]{strong="G3588 G3850" x-morph="robinson: T-ASF robinson: N-ASF 7 8"}; [A certain man planted a vineyard, and let it forth to husbandmen, and went into a far country for a long time.]{.wj} 
@10 [And at the season he sent a servant to the husbandmen, that they should give him of the fruit of the vineyard: but the husbandmen beat him, and sent him away empty.]{.wj} 
@11 [And again he sent another servant: and they beat him also, and entreated him shamefully, and sent him away empty.]{.wj} 
@12 [And again he sent a third: and they wounded him also, and cast him out.]{.wj} 
@13 [Then said the lord of the vineyard, What shall I do? I will send my beloved son: it may be they will reverence him when they see him.]{.wj} 
@14 [But when the husbandmen saw him, they reasoned among themselves, saying, This is the heir: come, let us kill him, that the inheritance may be ours.]{.wj} 
@15 [So they cast him out of the vineyard, and killed him. What therefore shall the lord of the vineyard do unto them?]{.wj}

@3 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 2"}, [Of a truth I say unto you, that this poor widow hath cast in more than they all:]{.wj} 
@4 [For all these have of their abundance cast in unto the offerings of God: but she of her penury hath cast in all the living that she had.]{.wj} 

@5 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [as some]{strong="G5100" x-morph="robinson: X-GPM 2"} [spake]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-PAP-GPM 3"} [of]{strong="G4012" x-morph="robinson: PREP 4"} [the temple]{strong="G3588 G2411" x-morph="robinson: T-GSN robinson: N-GSN 5 6"}, [how]{strong="G3754" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 7"} [it was adorned]{strong="G2885" x-morph="robinson: V-RPI-3S 12"} [with goodly]{strong="G2570" x-morph="robinson: A-DPM 9"} [stones]{strong="G3037" x-morph="robinson: N-DPM 8"} [and]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 10"} [gifts]{strong="G334" x-morph="robinson: N-DPN 11"}, [he said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 13"},

@8 [And]{strong="G1161" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 2"} [he said]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 3"}, [Take heed that ye be not deceived: for many shall come in my name, saying, I am Christ; and the time draweth near: go ye not therefore after them.]{.wj} 
@9 [But when ye shall hear of wars and commotions, be not terrified: for these things must first come to pass; but the end is not by and by.]{.wj} 
@10 [Then]{strong="G5119" x-morph="robinson: ADV 1"} [said he]{strong="G3004" x-morph="robinson: V-IAI-3S 2"} [unto them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 3"}, [Nation shall rise against nation, and kingdom against kingdom:]{.wj} 
@11 [And great earthquakes shall be in divers places, and famines, and pestilences; and fearful sights and great signs shall there be from heaven.]{.wj} 
@12 [But before all these, they shall lay their hands on you, and persecute you, delivering you up to the synagogues, and into prisons, being brought before kings and rulers for my name’s sake.]{.wj} 
@13 [And it shall turn to you for a testimony.]{.wj} 
@14 [Settle it therefore in your hearts, not to meditate before what ye shall answer:]{.wj} 
@15 [For I will give you a mouth and wisdom, which all your adversaries shall not be able to gainsay nor resist.]{.wj} 
@16 [And ye shall be betrayed both by parents, and brethren, and kinsfolks, and friends; and some of you shall they cause to be put to death.]{.wj} 
@17 [And ye shall be hated of all men for my name’s sake.]{.wj} 
@18 [But there shall not an hair of your head perish.]{.wj} 
@19 [In your patience possess ye your souls.]{.wj} 
@20 [And when ye shall see Jerusalem compassed with armies, then know that the desolation thereof is nigh.]{.wj} 
@21 [Then let them which are in Judæa flee to the mountains; and let them which are in the midst of it depart out; and let not them that are in the countries enter thereinto.]{.wj} 
@22 [For these be the days of vengeance, that all things which are written may be fulfilled.]{.wj} 
@23 [But woe unto them that are with child, and to them that give suck, in those days! for there shall be great distress in the land, and wrath upon this people.]{.wj} 
@24 [And they shall fall by the edge of the sword, and shall be led away captive into all nations: and Jerusalem shall be trodden down of the Gentiles, until the times of the Gentiles be fulfilled.]{.wj} 

@25 [And there shall be signs in the sun, and in the moon, and in the stars; and upon the earth distress of nations, with perplexity; the sea and the waves roaring;]{.wj} 
@26 [Men’s hearts failing them for fear, and for looking after those things which are coming on the earth: for the powers of heaven shall be shaken.]{.wj} 
@27 [And then shall they see the Son of man coming in a cloud with power and great glory.]{.wj} 
@28 [And when these things begin to come to pass, then look up, and lift up your heads; for your redemption draweth nigh.]{.wj} 
@29 [And]{strong="G2532" x-morph="robinson: CONJ 1"} [he spake]{strong="G2036" x-morph="robinson: V-2AAI-3S 2"} [to them]{strong="G846" x-morph="robinson: P-DPM 4"} [a parable]{strong="G3850" x-morph="robinson: N-ASF 3"}; [Behold the fig tree, and all the trees;]{.wj} 
@30 [When they now shoot forth, ye see and know of your own selves that summer is now nigh at hand.]{.wj} 
@31 [So likewise ye, when ye see these things come to pass, know ye that the kingdom of God is nigh at hand.]{.wj} 
@32 [Verily I say unto you, This generation shall not pass away, till all be fulfilled.]{.wj} 
@33 [Heaven and earth shall pass away: but my words shall not pass away.]{.wj} 

@34 [And take heed to yourselves, lest at any time your hearts be overcharged with surfeiting, and drunkenness, and cares of this life, and so that day come upon you unawares.]{.wj} 
@35 [For as a snare shall it come on all them that dwell on the face of the whole earth.]{.wj} 
@36 [Watch ye therefore, and pray always, that ye may be accounted worthy to escape all these things that shall come to pass, and to stand before the Son of man.]{.wj}

@29 [And I appoint unto you a kingdom, as my Father hath appointed unto me;]{.wj} 
@30 [That ye may eat and drink at my table in my kingdom, and sit on thrones judging the twelve tribes of Israel.]{.wj}
